          <dl>
            <dd>
              <small><b>Notes:</b></small>
              <dl>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    ><i>Date</i> is the "as of" date from the reference. A
                    single source may report statistics for multiple "as of"
                    dates.</small
                  >
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    >Total cases and deaths before 1 July 2014 are
                    calculated.</small
                  >
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    >Numbers with ± are deltas from a previous report. The
                    deltas may not be consistent.</small
                  >
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    >Numbers with a ↓ indicate cases that were
                    eliminated.</small
                  >
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small>Liberia:</small>
                  <dl>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >29 Mar: LI data is confused. Earlier, there were 8
                        suspected cases and 6 deaths (no confirmed cases). Seven
                        suspected cases were tested by 29 Mar, and five were not
                        Ebola. That should take suspected cases to 3, but a
                        total was not stated; it also implies deaths should be
                        at most 3. The report states only 2 suspected deaths
                        were tested, and one was not Ebola.<sup
                          id="cite_ref-30MAR14LI_288-1"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-30MAR14LI-288"
                            ><span>[</span>288<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >21 Apr: reduced deaths by 2: one in Guinea total and
                        one case discarded. 26 samples negative for Ebola.<sup
                          id="cite_ref-DON_22APR14_278-2"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-DON_22APR14-278"
                            ><span>[</span>278<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >24 Apr: stated it was reviewing its 27 suspected cases
                        and may toss all of them;<sup
                          id="cite_ref-DON_25APR14_277-2"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-DON_25APR14-277"
                            ><span>[</span>277<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >2 May: reclassification complete.<sup
                          id="cite_ref-DON_02MAY14_276-2"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-DON_02MAY14-276"
                            ><span>[</span>276<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                  </dl>
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    >Sierra Leone: cases were reported, but by 3 May there were
                    no cases.<sup
                      id="cite_ref-DON_05MAY14_275-1"
                      class="reference"
                      ><a href="#cite_note-DON_05MAY14-275"
                        ><span>[</span>275<span>]</span></a
                      ></sup
                    >
                    Early reports are marked with parens "()".</small
                  >
                  <dl>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >7 Apr: 2 suspected cases of EVD were confirmed as Lassa
                        Fever.<sup
                          id="cite_ref-DON_07APR14_284-1"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-DON_07APR14-284"
                            ><span>[</span>284<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                    <dd>
                      <small
                        >15 Apr: Of 12 suspected cases, 11 were tested for Ebola
                        but came up negative.<sup
                          id="cite_ref-DON_16APR14_281-1"
                          class="reference"
                          ><a href="#cite_note-DON_16APR14-281"
                            ><span>[</span>281<span>]</span></a
                          ></sup
                        ></small
                      >
                    </dd>
                  </dl>
                </dd>
                <dd>
                  <small
                    >Mali: 4 possible cases were reported on 7 April,<sup
                      id="cite_ref-DON_07APR14_284-2"
                      class="reference"
                      ><a href="#cite_note-DON_07APR14-284"
                        ><span>[</span>284<span>]</span></a
                      ></sup
                    ><sup id="cite_ref-DASH_07APR14_285-1" class="reference"
                      ><a href="#cite_note-DASH_07APR14-285"
                        ><span>[</span>285<span>]</span></a
                      ></sup
                    >
                    but they were not EVD.<sup
                      id="cite_ref-DON_16APR14_281-2"
                      class="reference"
                      ><a href="#cite_note-DON_16APR14-281"
                        ><span>[</span>281<span>]</span></a
                      ></sup
                    ></small
                  >
                </dd>
              </dl>
            </dd>
